[EVENT: PSE40171]
Date: 2023-11-05
Country: Palestine | Location: Nuba
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Palestine) [Rioters]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Ma'an News Agency; Newpress; Palestine News and Information Agency; PLO Negotiations Affairs Department [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 5 November 2023, Palestinian rioters threw rocks at Israeli forces during an Israeli raid in Nuba town (Hebron, West Bank). Israeli forces fired live and rubber bullets, stun grenades and tear gas canisters, killing 1 Palestinian man and injuring another. The forces arrested 12 workers from Gaza during the raid.
